ID_02427|The temperature in Danakil often exceeds fifty degrees Celsius, making it extremely hot throughout the year.
ID_02428|Salt mining has been practiced in Danakil for centuries, where workers cut salt blocks by hand.
ID_02429|Camel caravans transport the harvested salt blocks from the desert to the highlands of Ethiopia.
ID_02430|The vibrant yellow, green, and orange colors in Dallol are created by sulfur, iron, and mineral deposits.
ID_02431|Active volcanoes like Erta Ale are found in this region, featuring a persistent and bubbling lava lake.
ID_02432|Erta Ale's lava lake is one of the few permanent lava lakes existing continuously in the world today.
